   However to move mass from high S to low S, subtract proportional to S will reduce all, but low S less. After normalization, low S relatively increase, high decrease. Good.
   We can make it centroidal: subtract average S? a - step*(S - mean(S)) to preserve sum before norm and ensure zero-sum direction. ```

`direct_subgradient(a, idx, weights)`: idx array length G, weights normalized.
```
n=a.size; S=np.zeros(n,float64)
for pos,k in enumerate(idx):
   w=weights[pos]
   if w==0: continue
   lo=max(0,k-(n-1))
   hi=min(k,n-1)
   if hi>=lo:
      S[lo:hi+1] += w * a[hi:lo-1:-1]
return S
```
Weights sum1; if using weighted top values, normalized.

```
def polish_active(a, deadline, max_iters=1000, max_count=60, stop_stall=60):
    a = normalize1(a)
    best = a.copy(); best_score=compute_score(a)
    stall=0; variant=0; max_count_cur = 30
    target_m = best_score/(2*n)*0.999
    rel_step_scores = [0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.8]
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
       c = self_conv(a); M=c.max(); sc=2nM
       if sc<best_score - 1e-12: update; stall=0; target_m = max(best_score/(2n)*0.998, lower+eps)
       else: stall+=1
       # every 10 iter, if no improve, reduce max_count or change
       if stall>20: max_count_cur = 15; elif >40: max_count_cur=5; else ...
       D, kind = choose_direction(...)
       if D is None or D.std()<1e-15: break
       cand, cand_sc = line_search_mult(a,D, eta_grid)
       if cand_sc < best_score - 1e-12:
           best=cand; best_score=cand_sc; a=cand; stall=0
           # maybe lower target_m = best_score/(2n)*(1 - 0.001)
       elif cand_sc < best_score and stall<small: a=cand? Since cand_sc not <best? If best is a, cand_sc>=best no move.
       if stall>=stop_stall: break
    return best
```

Adam Flux top-level:
```
def adam_flux_polish(a0, deadline, beta=500, max_iters=3000, lr=0.05):
  z=np.log(np.clip(a0,1e-12,None)); z-=np.mean(z)
  m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n)
  best=...
  for it:
    sc,g=flux_lse_score_grad(z,beta)
    if sc<best: best...
    t++; bc1=1-b1**t; bc2=1-b2**t
    m=b1*m+(1-b1)*g; v=b2*v+(1-b2)*g*g
    z -= lr * (m/bc1)/(np.sqrt(v/bc2)+1e-8)
    # keep z centered and bounded
    z -= np.mean(z)
    z = np.clip(z, -50, 50)
  return best
```

```
def active_polish(a, deadline, target_score=None, max_iters=1000, base_max_count=40, eval_budget_per_iter=4):
    n=len(a); a=normalize1(a); best=a.copy(); best_sc=score
    stall=0; it=0; last_variation=0
    eta_short=[0.03,0.1,0.3]
    eta_short2=[0.01,0.05,0.2,0.5,1.0]
    while time<deadline and it<max_iters and stall<max_stall:
       c=self_conv(a); M=c.max(); sc=2nM
       if sc<best_sc - 1e-12: update
       else stall++
       # target_m
       if best_sc == prior: target_m = best_sc/(2n)*(1-0.0005)
       else target_m = best_sc/(2n)*(1-0.001)
       # if no active excess (c below target-m? impossible if target_m<best_m) 
       variant = it % 5
       if variant==0: G=20 uniform over excess
       elif variant==1: G=50 sqrt excess
       elif variant==2: G=100 rank
       elif variant==3: dense beta adaptive
       else: G=10 high rank
       D = choose_direction(...)
       scale=np.std(D); if scale small: break
       chosen candidate best among etas (maybe based on stall use extended etas when stall>5)
       if cand_sc < best_sc: a=cand; best=...; stall=0; maybe target much lower
       else:
          # If second best within 1e-9? no move.
          stall++
       it++
       if it % 20 ==0 print
    return best
```

Top-level `flux_lse_score_grad(a, beta)` maybe for a normalized:
```
def lse_score_grad(a,beta): # a sum1
   n=len(a); c=self_conv(a); Ssum=1; cmax=c.max(); z=beta*(c-cmax); z-=z.max(); p=exp(z)/sum; L=cmax+logsum/beta; score=2*n*L
   S=conv_transpose(a,p)
   grad_a = 4*n*S  # for score wrt a, no sum constraint? If a not constrained, yes. But for bounded L-BFGS, objective not enforced sum; we could normalize a before call. For L-BFGS bounded, if we normalize internally, grad wrt raw a includes scale derivative if not normalized. Easier use flux softmax objective. For a-space LBFGS, we can normalize in objective and gradient with quotient rule: score = 2nL(x)/S^2. But we can avoid by softmax.
```
For softmax objective, we need `flux_lse_score_grad_z(z,beta)`.

Implement `softmax_from_z(z)`:
```
z=np.asarray(z,float)
z-=z.max()
e=np.exp(z)
s=e.sum()
if s<=0: e=np.ones(len(z))/len(z)
return e/s
```
`flux_lse_score_grad_z(z,beta)`:
```
z=np.asarray(z)
n=z.size
a=softmax_from_z(z)
c=self_conv(a)
cmax=float(c.max())
zc=beta*(c-cmax); zc-=zc.max(); ex=np.exp(zc); exsum=ex.sum()
L=cmax+ np.log(exsum)/beta
score=2*n*L
# p = ex/exsum
S = conv_transpose(a, p) if exsum>0 else zeros
grad_a=4*n*S
mean = float(a.dot(grad_a))
grad_z = a*(grad_a - mean)
return float(score), grad_z
```

```
def line_search_mult(a,D,etas,current_score,max_mult=5.0):
    sc=float(np.sqrt(np.mean(D*D)))
    if sc<1e-15: return a,current_score
    rel=D/sc; rel=np.clip(rel,-8,8)
    best_cand=a; best_sc=current_score
    for eta in etas:
       mult=1-eta*rel; mult=clip(mult,0,max_mult)
       cand=a*mult; s=cand.sum(); if s<=0 continue; cand=cand/s
       sc=compute_score(cand)
       if sc<best_sc: best...
    return
```
This normalizes candidates. For self score, good. For cross line search, use different objective; implement `line_search_cross(a_fixed, y, D, etas, current_cross, max_mult)` similar but cross_conv_max.

12. `active_polish_self(a, deadline, max_iters=1000, base_max_count=40, stall_limit=80, verbose=False)`:
```
a=normalize1(a); n=len(a)
best=a.copy(); best_sc=compute_score(a)
stall=0; it=0
eta_short=(0.03,0.1,0.3,0.6)
eta_long=(0.01,0.05,0.15,0.4,0.8)
max_count_cycle=(20,50,100,30,200)
mode_cycle=('sqrt','rank','uniform','sqrt','rank')
while time<deadline and it<max_iters and stall<stall_limit:
   c=self_conv(a); M=c.max(); sc=2nM
   if sc < best_sc - 1e-12: best=a; best_sc=sc; stall=0
   else: stall+=1
   target_m = best_sc/(2*n)*(1-0.0008)
   # lower bound not below 1/(2n-1)
   lower_m=1.0/(2*n-1)
   if target_m < lower_m: target_m = lower_m*1.05
   variant=it % 5
   if variant == 4:
       D=make_direction_dense(a,c,target_m)
   else:
       mc = [20,40,80,12,200][variant]
       mode = ['sqrt','rank','uniform','rank','sqrt'][variant]
       idx,w=top_active_indices(c,target_m,mc,mode)
       D=make_direction_indices(a,idx,w)
   scale = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(D*D)))
   if scale < 1e-12:
       # maybe try dense
       D=make_direction_dense(...); scale=...
       if scale<1e-12: break
   etas = eta_short if stall<6 else eta_long
   cand,cand_sc=line_search_mult(a,D,etas,sc)
   if cand_sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
       a=cand; best=cand.copy(); best_sc=cand_sc; stall=0
       # if big improvement, maybe target_m= best_sc/(2n)*(1-0.002)
   else:
       # Optionally if cand_sc < sc? not possible unless best not a? best=a? 
       stall += 1
   it += 1
   if verbose and it%20==0: print
return best
```

Pseudo code full:
```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def pow2_len(m):
    m=int(m)
    if m<=1: return 1
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()

def normalize1(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size==0: return np.ones(1)
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
       return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)/a.size
    return a/s

def clean_sequence(seq): ... similar

def resample_sequence(a, n): ...

def self_conv(a): ...
def cross_conv(a,b): ...
def cross_conv_max(a,b): ... maybe similar but returns max
def conv_transpose(a,p): ...
def compute_score(a): ...

def softmax_from_z(z): ...
def flux_lse_score_grad_z(z, beta): ...

def top_active_indices(c, target, max_count, mode='sqrt'): ...
def direction_from_indices(a, idx, weights): ...
def direction_dense(a,c,target): ...
def line_search_mult_self(a, D, etas, current_score, max_mult=5.0): ...
def line_search_cross(a, y, D, etas, current_cross, max_mult=5.0): ...

def active_polish_self(a, deadline, max_iters=1000, stall_limit=80, max_count_base=40, verbose=False): ...
def cross_minimax_direction(a, deadline, max_iters=150, max_count=30, stall_limit=35): ...
def mix_accept(a, y, current_score, ts=(1.0,0.75,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.03)): ...
def random_smooth_sequence(n, rng): ...
def perturb_soft(a, rng, scale): ...
def run_flux_adam(a, deadline, betas=(100,300,1000), max_iters=300, lrs=(0.02,0.05,0.1)): ... maybe top loops; but to avoid too complex, implement one beta per call? Could.

```

`adam_flux_polish`:
```
def adam_flux_polish(a, beta, deadline, n_iters=1000, lr=0.05):
    a=normalize1(a); z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-12)); z-=z.mean()
    best=a.copy(); best_sc=compute_score(a)
    m=np.zeros_like(z); v=np.zeros_like(z); t=0
    b1=0.9; b2=0.999; eps=1e-8
    for it in range(n_iters):
       if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
       sc,g=flux_lse_score_grad_z(z,beta)
       if sc<best_sc-1e-12:
           a_cur=softmax_from_z(z); best=a_cur.copy(); best_sc=sc
       t+=1
       m=b1*m+(1-b1)*g
       v=b2*v+(1-b2)*(g*g)
       mb=m/(1-b1**t); vb=v/(1-b2**t)
       z = z - lr * (mb/(np.sqrt(vb)+eps))
       z = z - z.mean()
       # clip to avoid insane
       z = np.clip(z, -60, 60)
       a_cur=softmax_from_z(z) # maybe for next? Actually z already; no need.
    # final convert best already a
    return normalize1(best)
```

Let's write `active_polish_self` carefully:
```
def active_polish_self(a, deadline, max_iters=1000, stall_limit=80, verbose=False):
    a=normalize1(a); n=a.size
    if time.monotonic() >= deadline: return a
    c=self_conv(a); best=a.copy(); best_sc=float(2*n*c.max()/(a.sum()**2))
    stall=0; it=0
    etas_short=(0.03,0.1,0.3,0.6)
    etas_long=(0.01,0.05,0.15,0.4,0.8)
    counts=(20,40,80,12,150)
    modes=('sqrt','rank','uniform','rank','sqrt')
    while time.monotonic() < deadline and it < max_iters and stall < stall_limit:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
        c = self_conv(a)
        a_sum = 1.0 # normalized
        sc = float(2.0*n*c.max())
        if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best = a.copy(); best_sc = sc; stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
        M = float(c.max())
        target = M * 0.999
        lower = 1.0 / (2*n - 1)
        if target < lower * 1.01: target = lower * 1.01
        if it % 5 == 4:
            D = direction_dense(a, c, target)
        else:
            mc = counts[it % 5]
            mode = modes[it % 5]
            if mc > n: mc = n
            idx,w = top_active_indices(c, target, mc, mode)
            D = direction_from_indices(a, idx, w)
        dnorm = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(D*D)))
        if dnorm < 1e-14:
            D = direction_dense(a,c,target)
            dnorm = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(D*D)))
            if dnorm < 1e-14: break
        etas = etas_short if stall < 6 else etas_long
        cand, cand_sc = line_search_mult_self(a, D, etas, sc, 5.0)
        if cand_sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
            a = cand.copy(); best = cand.copy(); best_sc = cand_sc; stall = 0
        else:
            # if candidate better than current but not best? best==current likely; no move
            stall += 1
        it += 1
        if verbose and it % 50 == 0:
            print(f"    active self it={it} best={best_sc:.8f} stall={stall}")
    return best
```
Potential: We compute `sc` from current a; if current a has changed but not exact? We always set a=cand normalized; sc exact from line search? line_search returns cand_sc exact. Good. But after setting a, next loop recompute.

If `a` normalized sum1, score=2nM. Good.

`line_search_mult_self` current_score could be `sc` not best_sc? We want candidate better than current to accept? We compare cand_sc to best_sc. But if candidate worse than current but still <best_sc? impossible if best_sc==current. If previous best may not be current? In active_polish, current a is best (we only accept improved sets a=best). So okay. In line_search, we use current_score=sc. If no candidate < current, returns current, current score. Then no accept.

However if current `sc` is slightly higher than best_sc due numerical? We didn't set a=best if sc > best; a remains previous best? Actually if sc > best, stall increments and a not changed (unless previous candidate? a is best). So sc should equal best. Good.

`direction_from_indices`: It subtracts mean; if idx/w all zero? weights normalized.
```
def direction_from_indices(a, idx, weights):
    n=a.size; S=np.zeros(n)
    for k,w in zip(idx,weights):
       if w==0: continue
       k=int(k)
       lo = 0 if k > n-1? Actually lo=max(0,k-n+1), hi=min(k,n-1)
       if hi>=lo:
           S[lo:hi+1] += w * a[hi:lo-1:-1]
    return S - S.mean()
```
Check slice `a[hi:lo-1:-1]` if lo=0, hi=n-1 => a[n-1:-1:-1] gives length n-1? Wait stop exclusive -1 means stop at index1? For lo=0, want a[hi], a[hi-1], ..., a[0]. Slice `a[hi::-1]` works. General: `a[hi:lo-1:-1]`. If lo=0 => `a[hi:-1:-1]` stops at -1 (exclusive index -1), so index 1? That excludes 0! In Python negative stop tricky. Better use:
```
S[lo:hi+1] += w * a[hi:lo-1:-1] if lo > 0 else w * a[hi::-1]
```
Or use fancy indexing: `np.flip(a[lo:hi+1])`? We need a[hi:lo-1:-1]. Since lo>=0, `a[hi:lo-1:-1]` when lo=0 gives `a[hi:-1:-1]` which stops before -1 = index? Example a length5, hi=4, lo=0: a[4:-1:-1] -> indices 4,3,2,1 (stops at 0?) Actually -1 is last index 4? Wait step negative, stop -1 means index length-1=4; start4, next3,2,1, then next0 which is <= stop? For negative step, slice continues while index > stop. stop= -1 means index > 4? No, Python normalizes stop to 4? For a[4:-1:-1], sequence length 0? Let's test mentally: `[::-1]` full uses stop None. `a[4:-1:-1]` probably yields index4 only? Because stop -1 refers to last element which is start, so stops immediately? Better avoid. Use `a[lo:hi+1][::-1]` allocates slice but length up to n, fine. Or `a[np.arange(hi, lo-1, -1)]`. Use `seg = a[lo:hi+1]; S[lo:hi+1] += w * seg[::-1]`. Simple.

Weights maybe float; if idx unsorted, no issue.

`top_active_indices`: Use `np.float64`. For rank weights, need sort descending. Implement:
```
def top_active_indices(c, target, max_count, mode):
    L=len(c)
    if max_count > L: max_count=L
    if max_count <=0: return np.array([],dtype=int), np.array([],float)
    excess = c - target
    active = np.nonzero(excess > 0)[0]
    if active.size == 0:
        if max_count >= L: idx=np.arange(L)
        else: idx=np.argpartition(c, L-max_count)[L-max_count:]
        vals = c[idx]
    else:
        if active.size > max_count:
           e_active = excess[active]
           part = np.argpartition(e_active, active.size - max_count)[active.size - max_count:]
           idx = active[part]
        else:
           idx = active
        vals = excess[idx]
    if vals.size != max_count and vals.size>0 and mode=='rank': pass
    if stereo? 
    if mode == 'uniform' or not np.any(vals > 0):
       # sort idx descending for deterministic?
       order=np.argsort(c[idx])[::-1]
       idx=idx[order]
       w=np.ones(idx.size)
    elif mode == 'rank':
       order=np.argsort(vals)[::-1]
       idx=idx[order]; vals=vals[order]
       G=idx.size
       w=np.arange(G,0,-1,dtype=np.float64)
       w = 1.0 + 0.5*w # avoid zero
       w /= w.sum()
    else: # sqrt
       order=np.argsort(vals)[::-1]
       idx=idx[order]; vals=vals[order]
       w=np.sqrt(np.maximum(vals,0.0)) + 1e-12
       w /= w.sum()
    return idx,w
```
If vals all very small, sqrt okay. For active size > max_count, `argpartition` kth = active.size - max_count. If max_count=0 no.
If active size less than max_count, idx size active size, not max_count. Good.
If `excess` all <=0, idx top by c; vals=c[idx] not excess; weights rank/sqrt okay.

`run_flux_lbfgs`: Need if SciPy minimize fails? Use try except.
```
def run_flux_lbfgs(a, deadline, betas=(100,300,1000,3000), verbose=False):
   a=normalize1(a); best=a.copy(); best_sc=compute_score(a)
   for beta in betas:
      now=time.monotonic()
      if now>deadline: break
      rem=deadline-now
      maxfun=int(min(600, max(150, rem/5.0)))
      z=np.log(np.maximum(best,1e-12)); z-=z.mean()
      try:
         res=minimize(flux_lse_score_grad_z, z, args=(beta,), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True,
                      options={'maxiter':maxfun//10, 'maxfun':maxfun, 'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-8,'maxls':20})
         z2=res.x; a2=normalize1(softmax_from_z(z2)); sc2=compute_score(a2)
         if sc2<best_sc:
            best=a2.copy(); best_sc=sc2
            if verbose: print
      except Exception as e:
         if verbose: print('LBFGS fail', e)
   return best
```
`maxiter` at least 1. The L-BFGS maxfun counts function evals; each eval returns f,g. Good.

Could L-BFGS with n=7000 and maxfun 600 for each beta take too long? Each eval maybe 0.02-0.05s; 4*600=2400 evals ->100s. okay.

```
def propose_candidate(*args, **kwargs):
   budget_s = float(kwargs.get('budget_s', 2400.0))
   t0=time.monotonic()
   total = max(60.0,budget_s) - 45.0
   hard = t0+total
   rng = np.random.default_rng(777)
   # load parent
   print start
   parent=None
   try:
      val=globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
      if val is not None:
         p=clean_sequence(val)
         if p.size>=32: parent=p
   except: pass
   if parent is None or parent.size==0:
       n=4096
       best=np.ones(n)/n
   else:
       if parent.size>15000:
          parent=resample_sequence(parent, min(8192, parent.size)) # careful resample_sequence expects target? define resample_sequence(a, new_n)
       best=normalize1(parent)
   best_sc=compute_score(best)
   print best_sc

   # phase1 quick starts
   phase1_end = t0 + 0.15*total
   starts=[]; starts.append(best.copy())
   n=best.size
   starts.append(np.ones(n)/n)
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   starts.append(normalize1(np.clip(0.5 + 0.7*x + 0.2*(1-x)**2, 0.01, None)))
   starts.append(normalize1(np.clip(0.6 + 0.25*np.sin(2*np.pi*x) + 0.1*np.cos(4*np.pi*x+0.5), 0.01, None)))
   starts.append(random_smooth_sequence(n,rng))
   # for each unique start maybe up to 5, run active_polish with chunk time
   for st in starts:
      if time.monotonic() >= phase1_end: break
      st=normalize1(st)
      st_sc=compute_score(st)
      if st_sc < best_sc: best=st; best_sc=st_sc
      chunk=min(120.0, (phase1_end - time.monotonic())*0.7)
      # if chunk < 20 break
      a2=active_polish_self(st, time.monotonic()+chunk, max_iters=300, stall_limit=30, verbose=False)
      sc2=compute_score(a2)
      if sc2<best_sc: best=a2; best_sc=sc2
   print after phase1

   # phase2 cycles
   phase2_end = t0 + 0.82*total
   no_cycle_improve=0
   cycle=0
   while time.monotonic() < phase2_end:
      cycle +=1
      base_score=best_sc
      a=normalize1(best)
      # chunk sizes
      c_time = time.monotonic()
      t1_end = min(phase2_end, max(c_time+30, c_time + 0.30*(phase2_end-c_time)))
      a2=active_polish_self(a, t1_end, max_iters=1000, stall_limit=40, verbose=True)
      sc2=compute_score(a2); improve=False
      if sc2 < best_sc - 1e-12: best=a2; best_sc=sc2; improve=True
      # cross if time

      y=cross_minimax_direction(normalize1(best), t2_end, max_iters=150, stall_limit=30)
      a3, sc3=mix_accept(best, y, best_sc)
      if sc3 < best_sc - 1e-12: best=a3; best_sc=sc3; improve=True
      # self after mix
      t3_end = min(phase2_end, max(time.monotonic()+30, time.monotonic() + 0.45*(phase2_end-time.monotonic())))
      a4=active_polish_self(normalize1(best), t3_end, max_iters=1500, stall_limit=60, verbose=True)
      sc4=compute_score(a4)
      if sc4 < best_sc - 1e-12: best=a4; best_sc=sc4; improve=True
      if improve: no_cycle_improve=0
      else:
         no_cycle_improve +=1
         # perturb
         t4_end = min(phase2_end, time.monotonic()+80)
         if time.monotonic() < t4_end:
            pert=perturb_soft(normalize1(best), rng, 0.1 + 0.05*no_cycle_improve)
            a5=active_polish_self(pert, t4_end, max_iters=300, stall_limit=30)
            sc5=compute_score(a5)
            if sc5 < best_sc-1e-12: best=a5; best_sc=sc5; no_cycle_improve=0; improve=True
      print cycle best
      if no_cycle_improve >=3: break
   print after phase2

   # final polish
   final_end = hard - 10
   if time.monotonic() < final_end:
      # active final

      a9=active_polish_self(normalize1(best), min(final_end, time.monotonic()+40), max_iters=200, stall_limit=50)
      update

   out=[float(max(0.0,min(1000.0,x))) for x in best]
   if len(out)==0 or sum(out)<0.01: out=[0.5]*64
   # verify

   return out
```

Potential issue with L-BFGS unbounded and `jac=True`: The gradient `grad_z` has sum zero; but objective invariant to adding constant. L-BFGS may try to move in constant direction? gradient zero there, okay. Centering z inside function makes gradient with respect to uncentered? Let's detail `flux_lse_score_grad_z`:
```
def flux_lse_score_grad_z(z, beta):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = z.size
    zz = z - float(np.mean(z))
    zz = np.clip(zz, -80.0, 80.0)  # to keep exp stable? Row bounds may create kinks; but 80 large enough. Adding clip might zero gradient at bounds? If values beyond, objective flat; okay. Maybe skip clip unless necessary. Use clip to avoid overflow in exp after max? Exp after max shift, values can be -huge no overflow. Positive differences zero (max subtract), no overflow. So no clip needed. But if zz has inf, clip. Use `zz = np.where(np.isfinite(zz), zz, 0.0)`. Keep diff.
    a = softmax_from_z(zz)
    c = self_conv(a)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    zc = beta*(c-cmax)
    zc = zc - zc.max()
    ex = np.exp(zc)
    exsum = float(ex.sum())
    if exsum <= 0: p = np.ones_like(c)/c.size; L=cmax
    else: p=ex/exsum; L=cmax + np.log(exsum)/beta
    score = 2.0*n*L
    S = conv_transpose(a, p)
    grad_a = 4.0*n*S
    meanag = float(np.dot(a, grad_a))
    grad_z = a*(grad_a - meanag)
    return float(score), grad_z
```
Should `L` include cmax? Yes LSE = max + logsumexp(β(c-max))/β. If exsum after shift: logsum = zc.max() - shift + log(exsum) = logsum of exp(β(c-cmax)). `zc - zc.max()` then log(exsum) plus zc.max(). So `L = cmax + (zc_max_orig + log(exsum))/beta`. We omitted `zc_max_orig`. Since we compute zc=β(c-cmax); zc_max=zc.max() (could be 0 if cmax attained; due floating maybe 0). But if we subtract zc.max before exp, L = cmax + (zc_max + log(exsum))/beta. We need store `zc_max = float(zc.max())`, then `zc_shifted=zc-zc_max`. If cmax is actual max, zc_max=0, but due cmax maybe c.max(), yes. However if c contains cmax exactly, zc_max=0. For safety, use zc_max. Then `L = cmax + (zc_max + np.log(exsum))/beta`. But if cmax is c.max(), zc_max=0. Good.
Alternatively LSE of c: (1/β)log∑e^{βc} = cmax + (1/β)(zc_max + log(sum exp(zc-zc_max))). We'll implement with zc_max.

`p=exp(zc-zc_max)/sum`.

Now, `softmax_from_z` should also center? It can accept any. `zz = z - np.max(z)` but if z has inf? If input finite. Use:
```
def softmax_from_z(z):
   z=np.asarray(z,float).ravel()
   z=np.where(np.isfinite(z),z,0.0)
   m=float(np.max(z)); e=np.exp(z-m); s=e.sum(); if s<=0: e=np.ones_like(z)/z.size; return e/s
```
In L-BFGS, if z-centered and clipped, okay.

`make_direction_dense` p LSE: similar compute zc_max to normalize p. If beta infinite? no.

Now, exact `active_polish_self` target: after score improved, best_sc updated. But if candidate score lower but due normalization? good.

Let's think if active_polish with line search etas could get stuck because candidate score computed after normalization but D direction was based on current normalized a. If candidate multipliers can increase sum significantly, normalization effectively also moves along scaling, but score invariant; however the shape after normalization is exactly mult/s. Good.

```
   b=np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
```

```python
"""
Optimizer for sequences minimizing
    2*n*max(a*a)/(sum(a)^2)
where a*a is the full linear convolution (polynomial square) of a.

The main ingredients are:
- FFT-based convolution and gradients.
- An active-subgradient multiplicative update driven by the currently
  largest coefficients of a*a.
- A cross-convolution minimax step (equivalent to the LP subproblem in the
  known alternating method), used as a stronger direction.
- Optional smoothed log-sum-exp polishing in log-softmax variables.

All helper functions are top-level; no closures or lambdas are used.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def pow2_len(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def clean_sequence(seq):
    """Convert input to a finite non-negative float vector."""
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return arr


def normalize1(a):
    """Normalize to sum 1, keeping only finite non-negative values."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / a.size
    return a / s


def resample_sequence(a, new_n):
    """Linear resampling to a new length."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    old_n = a.size
    if old_n == 0:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / new_n
    if new_n == old_n:
        return a.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, old_n - 1.0, old_n)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, new_n - 1.0, new_n)
    b = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    return np.maximum(b, 0.0)


def self_conv(a):
    """Full linear convolution a*a using FFT."""
    n = int(np.size(a))
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    N = pow2_len(m)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:m]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    return np.maximum(c, 0.0)


def cross_conv(a, b):
    """Full linear convolution a*b using FFT."""
    n = int(np.size(a))
    m = int(np.size(b))
    if n == 0 or m == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    L = n + m - 1
    N = pow2_len(L)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    B = np.fft.rfft(b, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * B, N)[:L]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    return np.maximum(c, 0.0)


def cross_conv_max(a, b):
    """Return max of full linear convolution a*b, without returning the array."""
    n = int(np.size(a))
    m = int(np.size(b))
    if n == 0 or m == 0:
        return 0.0
    L = n + m - 1
    N = pow2_len(L)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    B = np.fft.rfft(b, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * B, N)[:L]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    return float(np.max(c))


def conv_transpose(a, p):
    """
    For c_k = sum_j a_{k-j} y_j, compute the gradient-like vector
        v_i = sum_k p_k a_{k-i}
    for i = 0,...,n-1, where p has length 2*n-1.
    """
    n = int(np.size(a))
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    L = 3 * n - 2
    N = pow2_len(L)
    ra = a[::-1]
    A = np.fft.rfft(ra, N)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * P, N)
    out = c[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    return np.maximum(out, 0.0)


def compute_score(a):
    """Evaluate the internal objective."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return np.inf
    c = self_conv(a)
    mx = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * mx / (s * s))


def softmax_from_z(z):
    """Stable softmax from log-variables."""
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if z.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    m = float(np.max(z))
    e = np.exp(z - m)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        e = np.ones(z.size, dtype=np.float64)
        return e / z.size
    return e / s


def flux_lse_score_grad_z(z, beta):
    """
    Smoothed log-sum-exp objective on softmax variables z.
    Returns (score, grad_z).
    """
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = z.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf, np.ones(0, dtype=np.float64)

    zz = z - float(np.mean(z))
    zz = np.where(np.isfinite(zz), zz, 0.0)
    a = softmax_from_z(zz)
    c = self_conv(a)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))

    zc = beta * (c - cmax)
    zc_max = float(np.max(zc))
    zc = zc - zc_max
    ex = np.exp(zc)
    exsum = float(ex.sum())
    if exsum <= 1e-300:
        p = np.ones_like(c, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, c.size)
        Lval = cmax
    else:
        p = ex / exsum
        Lval = cmax + (zc_max + float(np.log(exsum))) / beta

    score = 2.0 * n * Lval

    S = conv_transpose(a, p)
    grad_a = 4.0 * n * S
    mean_ag = float(np.dot(a, grad_a))
    grad_z = a * (grad_a - mean_ag)
    grad_z = np.where(np.isfinite(grad_z), grad_z, 0.0)
    return float(score), grad_z


def top_active_indices(c, target, max_count, mode="sqrt"):
    """
    Return indices and weights of the most violated coefficients.
    The active set is based on c_k > target, falling back to top coefficients.
    """
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    L = c.size
    if L == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=int), np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)

    max_count = int(max_count)
    if max_count <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=int), np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if max_count > L:
        max_count = L

    excess = c - target
    active = np.nonzero(excess > 0.0)[0]

    if active.size == 0:
        if max_count >= L:
            idx = np.arange(L, dtype=int)
        else:
            idx = np.argpartition(c, L - max_count)[L - max_count:].astype(int)
        vals = c[idx]
    else:
        if active.size > max_count:
            e_act = excess[active]
            part = np.argpartition(e_act, active.size - max_count)[active.size - max_count:]
            idx = active[part].astype(int)
        else:
            idx = active.astype(int)
        vals = c[idx]

    if idx.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=int), np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)

    if mode == "uniform" or not np.any(vals > target):
        order = np.argsort(c[idx])[::-1]
        idx = idx[order]
        w = np.ones(idx.size, dtype=np.float64)
        w /= w.sum()
        return idx, w

    if mode == "rank":
        order = np.argsort(vals)[::-1]
        idx = idx[order]
        G = idx.size
        w = np.arange(G, 1, -1, dtype=np.float64)
        w = 1.0 + 0.5 * w
        w /= w.sum()
        return idx, w

    order = np.argsort(vals)[::-1]
    idx = idx[order]
    vals = np.maximum(vals, 0.0)
    w = np.sqrt(vals) + 1e-12
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        w = np.ones(idx.size, dtype=np.float64)
        return idx, w / idx.size
    return idx, w / s


def direction_from_indices(a, idx, weights):
    """
    Compute centered multiplier direction
        D_i = sum_k weights_k * a_{k-i} - mean(...).
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    S = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if idx is None or len(idx) == 0:
        return S
    if weights is None or len(weights) == 0:
        return S

    for k, w in zip(idx, weights):
        if w == 0.0:
            continue
        k = int(k)
        lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
        hi = min(k, n - 1)
        if hi >= lo:
            S[lo:hi + 1] += float(w) * a[lo:hi + 1][::-1]

    S = np.where(np.isfinite(S), S, 0.0)
    return S - float(np.mean(S))


def direction_dense(a, c, target):
    """Dense LSE-based direction over all convolution coefficients."""
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    L = c.size
    if L == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)

    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    span = max(cmax - float(target), 1e-15)
    beta = min(1.0e6, 50.0 / span)

    zc = beta * (c - cmax)
    zc_max = float(np.max(zc))
    zc = zc - zc_max
    ex = np.exp(zc)
    exsum = float(ex.sum())
    if exsum <= 1e-300:
        p = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / L
    else:
        p = ex / exsum

    S = conv_transpose(a, p)
    S = np.where(np.isfinite(S), S, 0.0)
    return S - float(np.mean(S))


def line_search_mult_self(a, D, etas, current_score, max_mult=5.0):
    """
    Search multiplicative updates
        a * (1 - eta * standardized D)
    over a small set of eta values.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    D = np.asarray(D, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    best_cand = a.copy()
    best_sc = float(current_score)

    dnorm = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(D * D)))
    if not np.isfinite(dnorm) or dnorm <= 1e-15:
        return best_cand, best_sc

    rel = D / dnorm
    rel = np.clip(rel, -8.0, 8.0)

    for eta in etas:
        mult = 1.0 - float(eta) * rel
        mult = np.clip(mult, 0.0, float(max_mult))
        cand = a * mult
        cand = np.where(np.isfinite(cand), cand, 0.0)
        cand = np.clip(cand, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(cand.sum())
        if s <= 1e-12:
            continue
        cand = cand / s
        sc = compute_score(cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_cand = cand.copy()
    return best_cand, best_sc


def line_search_cross(a_fixed, y, D, etas, current_cross, max_mult=5.0):
    """Search for y in multiplicative direction to lower max(a_fixed*y)."""
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    D = np.asarray(D, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    best_y = y.copy()
    best_sc = float(current_cross)

    dnorm = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(D * D)))
    if not np.isfinite(dnorm) or dnorm <= 1e-15:
        return best_y, best_sc

    rel = D / dnorm
    rel = np.clip(rel, -8.0, 8.0)

    for eta in etas:
        mult = 1.0 - float(eta) * rel
        mult = np.clip(mult, 0.0, float(max_mult))
        cand = y * mult
        cand = np.where(np.isfinite(cand), cand, 0.0)
        cand = np.clip(cand, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(cand.sum())
        if s <= 1e-12:
            continue
        cand = cand / s
        sc = cross_conv_max(a_fixed, cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_y = cand.copy()
    return best_y, best_sc


def active_polish_self(a, deadline, max_iters=1000, stall_limit=80, verbose=False):
    """
    Monotone active-subgradient polish on the self-convolution objective.
    """
    a = normalize1(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a, np.inf

    c = self_conv(a)
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = float(2.0 * n * c.max())

    stall = 0
    it = 0

    etas_short = (0.03, 0.10, 0.30, 0.60)
    etas_long = (0.01, 0.05, 0.15, 0.40, 0.80)

    counts = (8, 30, 80, 3, 200)
    modes = ("rank", "sqrt", "uniform", "rank", "sqrt")

    while time.monotonic() < deadline and it < max_iters and stall < stall_limit:
        c = self_conv(a)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s <= 1e-12:
            break
        sc = float(2.0 * n * float(np.max(c)) / (s * s))

        if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best = a.copy()
            best_sc = sc
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1

        M = float(np.max(c))
        target = 0.999 * M
        lower = 1.0 / (2 * n - 1)
        if target < 1.01 * lower:
            target = 1.01 * lower

        variant = it % 5
        if variant == 4:
            D = direction_dense(a, c, target)
        else:
            mc = counts[variant]
            mode = modes[variant]
            if mc > n:
                mc = n
            idx, w = top_active_indices(c, target, mc, mode)
            D = direction_from_indices(a, idx, w)

        dnorm = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(D * D)))
        if not np.isfinite(dnorm) or dnorm <= 1e-14:
            D = direction_dense(a, c, target)
            dnorm = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(D * D)))
            if not np.isfinite(dnorm) or dnorm <= 1e-14:
                break

        etas = etas_short if stall < 6 else etas_long
        cand, cand_sc = line_search_mult_self(a, D, etas, sc, max_mult=5.0)

        if cand_sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
            a = cand.copy()
            best = cand.copy()
            best_sc = cand_sc
            stall = 0
        # otherwise stall remains incremented

        it += 1
        if verbose and (it % 50 == 0):
            print(f"      active-self it={it} best={best_sc:.8f} stall={stall}")

    return best, best_sc


def cross_minimax_direction(a, deadline, max_iters=150, max_count=30, stall_limit=35):
    """
    Find y minimizing max(a*y) approximately using multiplicative subgradient
    steps. Returns best y found.
    """
    a = normalize1(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return normalize1(np.ones(1))

    y = normalize1(a.copy())
    r = cross_conv(a, y)
    best_y = y.copy()
    best_cross = float(np.max(r))

    stall = 0
    it = 0
    lower = 1.0 / (2 * n - 1)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline and it < max_iters and stall < stall_limit:
        r = cross_conv(a, y)
        M = float(np.max(r))
        if M < best_cross - 1e-12:
            best_y = y.copy()
            best_cross = M
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1

        target = 0.999 * best_cross
        if target < 1.01 * lower:
            target = 1.01 * lower

        mode = "sqrt" if (it % 2 == 0) else "rank"
        idx, w = top_active_indices(r, target, max_count, mode)
        D = direction_from_indices(a, idx, w)

        dnorm = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(D * D)))
        if not np.isfinite(dnorm) or dnorm <= 1e-14:
            D = direction_dense(a, r, target)
            dnorm = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(D * D)))
            if not np.isfinite(dnorm) or dnorm <= 1e-14:
                break

        if stall < 5:
            etas = (0.02, 0.08, 0.25, 0.60)
        else:
            etas = (0.005, 0.02, 0.08, 0.20, 0.50)

        y_new, cross_new = line_search_cross(a, y, D, etas, M, max_mult=5.0)
        if cross_new < M - 1e-13:
            y = y_new.copy()
            stall = 0
        else:
            # stall was already incremented above
            pass

        it += 1

    return best_y


def mix_accept(a, y, current_score, ts=(1.0, 0.75, 0.50, 0.25, 0.10, 0.03)):
    """Try convex combinations of a and y and return best by exact score."""
    a = normalize1(a)
    y = normalize1(y)
    best_cand = a.copy()
    best_sc = float(current_score)

    for t in ts:
        if t <= 0.0:
            cand = a.copy()
            sc = best_sc
        else:
            cand = (1.0 - t) * a + t * y
            cand = np.where(np.isfinite(cand), cand, 0.0)
            cand = np.clip(cand, 0.0, 1000.0)
            s = float(cand.sum())
            if s <= 1e-12:
                continue
            cand = cand / s
            sc = compute_score(cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
            best_sc = sc
            best_cand = cand.copy()

    return best_cand, best_sc


def random_smooth_sequence(n, rng):
    """Random smooth nonnegative initialization from low-pass noise."""
    z = rng.standard_normal(n)
    N = pow2_len(n)
    F = np.fft.rfft(z, N)
    F[3:] = 0.0
    dp = np.fft.irfft(F, N)[:n]
    std = float(np.std(dp))
    if std <= 1e-12:
        dp = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        dp = dp / std
    a = 1.0 + 0.25 * dp
    a = np.clip(a, 0.05, 5.0)
    return normalize1(a)


def perturb_soft(a, rng, scale):
    """Low-frequency log-perturbation to escape local minima."""
    a = normalize1(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a
    z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-12))
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))

    noise = rng.standard_normal(n)
    N = pow2_len(n)
    F = np.fft.rfft(noise, N)
    F[4:] = 0.0
    dp = np.fft.irfft(F, N)[:n]
    dp = dp - float(np.mean(dp))
    std = float(np.std(dp))
    if std <= 1e-12:
        return normalize1(softmax_from_z(z))
    z = z + float(scale) * dp / std
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    return normalize1(softmax_from_z(z))


def run_flux_lbfgs(a, deadline, betas=(100.0, 300.0, 1000.0, 3000.0), verbose=False):
    """Smoothed LSE L-BFGS polishing in log-softmax variables."""
    a = normalize1(a)
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = compute_score(a)

    for beta in betas:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= deadline:
            break
        remaining = float(deadline - now)
        if remaining < 3.0:
            break

        maxfun = int(min(350, max(80, remaining / 6.0)))
        maxiter = max(20, int(maxfun // 5))

        z = np.log(np.maximum(best, 1e-12))
        z = z - float(np.mean(z))
        z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)

        try:
            res = minimize(
                flux_lse_score_grad_z,
                z,
                args=(float(beta),),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                jac=True,
                options={
                    "ftol": 1e-12,
                    "gtol": 1e-8,
                    "maxiter": maxiter,
                    "maxfun": maxfun,
                    "maxls": 20,
                }
            )
            if hasattr(res, "x"):
                z2 = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
                z2 = np.where(np.isfinite(z2), z2, 0.0)
                a2 = normalize1(softmax_from_z(z2))
                sc2 = compute_score(a2)
                if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc - 1e-13:
                    best = a2.copy()
                    best_sc = sc2
                    if verbose:
                        print(f"      LBFGS beta={beta:.0f} improved to {best_sc:.8f}")
        except Exception as exc:
            if verbose:
                print("      LBFGS exception:", exc)

    return best, best_sc


def adam_flux_polish(a, beta, deadline, max_iters=500, lr=0.05, verbose=False):
    """Adam smoothing-polish in log-softmax variables."""
    a = normalize1(a)
    z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-12))
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)

    best_z = z.copy()
    best_a = normalize1(softmax_from_z(best_z))
    best_sc = compute_score(best_a)

    n = z.size
    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b1 = 0.90
    b2 = 0.999
    eps = 1e-8
    t = 0

    for _ in range(int(max_iters)):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        sc, g = flux_lse_score_grad_z(z, float(beta))
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            break
        if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_z = z.copy()
            best_a = normalize1(softmax_from_z(best_z))
            best_sc = sc

        t += 1
        m = b1 * m + (1.0 - b1) * g
        v = b2 * v + (1.0 - b2) * (g * g)
        mb = m / (1.0 - b1 ** t)
        vb = v / (1.0 - b2 ** t)
        z = z - float(lr) * mb / (np.sqrt(vb) + eps)
        z = z - float(np.mean(z))
        z = np.clip(z, -80.0, 80.0)
        z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)

    if verbose:
        print(f"      Adam LSE beta={beta:.0f} improved to {best_sc:.8f}")
    return best_a, best_sc


def propose_candidate(*args, **kwargs):
    budget_s = 2400.0
    if "budget_s" in kwargs:
        try:
            budget_s = float(kwargs["budget_s"])
        except Exception:
            budget_s = 2400.0
    elif len(args) > 0 and isinstance(args[0], (int, float, np.integer, np.floating)):
        try:
            budget_s = float(args[0])
        except Exception:
            budget_s = 2400.0

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = max(60.0, float(budget_s)) - 45.0
    if total_budget < 60.0:
        total_budget = 60.0
    hard_deadline = t0 + total_budget
    rng = np.random.default_rng(81237)

    print(f"Starting search, budget={budget_s:.1f}s, usable~{total_budget:.1f}s")

    parent = None
    try:
        val = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if val is not None:
            p = clean_sequence(val)
            if p.size >= 32:
                parent = p
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is None or parent.size == 0:
        n = 4096
        best = normalize1(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
        print("No parent available; using generic start.")
    else:
        if parent.size > 15000:
            parent = resample_sequence(parent, 8192)
        best = normalize1(parent)
        print(f"Loaded parent length={parent.size}, score={compute_score(best):.8f}")

    best_sc = compute_score(best)
    print(f"Initial best score = {best_sc:.8f}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 1: quick diversity search
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    phase1_end = t0 + 0.15 * total_budget
    n = best.size
    starts = [best.copy(), normalize1(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))]
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    starts.append(normalize1(np.clip(0.5 + 0.7 * x + 0.2 * (1.0 - x) ** 2, 1e-3, None)))
    starts.append(normalize1(
        np.clip(0.6 + 0.25 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x) + 0.10 * np.cos(4.0 * np.pi * x + 0.5), 1e-3, None)
    ))
    starts.append(random_smooth_sequence(n, rng))

    for st in starts:
        if time.monotonic() >= phase1_end:
            break
        st = normalize1(st)
        st_sc = compute_score(st)
        if np.isfinite(st_sc) and st_sc < best_sc:
            best = st.copy()
            best_sc = st_sc

        chunk = min(120.0, max(2.0, (phase1_end - time.monotonic()) * 0.7))
        if time.monotonic() < phase1_end - 2.0:
            a2, sc2 = active_polish_self(st, time.monotonic() + chunk, max_iters=250,
                                         stall_limit=30, verbose=False)
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best = a2.copy()
                best_sc = sc2

    print(f"After phase 1: best = {best_sc:.8f} (t={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s)")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 2: main alternating search
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    phase2_end = t0 + 0.82 * total_budget
    no_cycle_improve = 0
    cycle = 0

    while time.monotonic() < phase2_end:
        cycle += 1
        base_score = best_sc
        improved = False

        # Self-active polish.
        rem = phase2_end - time.monotonic()
        t1_end = min(phase2_end, time.monotonic() + max(30.0, 0.30 * rem))
        a1, sc1 = active_polish_self(normalize1(best), t1_end,
                                     max_iters=1200, stall_limit=50, verbose=True)
        if np.isfinite(sc1) and sc1 < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best = a1.copy()
            best_sc = sc1
            improved = True

        # Cross minmix direction and mixing.
        if time.monotonic() < phase2_end:
            rem = phase2_end - time.monotonic()
            t2_end = min(phase2_end, time.monotonic() + max(25.0, 0.22 * rem))
            y = cross_minimax_direction(normalize1(best), t2_end,
                                        max_iters=130, max_count=30, stall_limit=30)
            a2, sc2 = mix_accept(normalize1(best), y, best_sc)
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best = a2.copy()
                best_sc = sc2
                improved = True

        # More self-active polish after mixing.
        if time.monotonic() < phase2_end:
            rem = phase2_end - time.monotonic()
            t3_end = min(phase2_end, time.monotonic() + max(30.0, 0.45 * rem))
            a3, sc3 = active_polish_self(normalize1(best), t3_end,
                                         max_iters=2500, stall_limit=80, verbose=True)
            if np.isfinite(sc3) and sc3 < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best = a3.copy()
                best_sc = sc3
                improved = True

        if improved:
            no_cycle_improve = 0
        else:
            no_cycle_improve += 1
            if time.monotonic() < phase2_end - 30.0:
                pert = perturb_soft(normalize1(best), rng, 0.08 + 0.05 * no_cycle_improve)
                t4_end = min(phase2_end, time.monotonic() + 80.0)
                a4, sc4 = active_polish_self(pert, t4_end, max_iters=350,
                                             stall_limit=35, verbose=False)
                if np.isfinite(sc4) and sc4 < best_sc - 1e-12:
                    best = a4.copy()
                    best_sc = sc4
                    no_cycle_improve = 0
                    improved = True

        print(f"  cycle {cycle}: best = {best_sc:.8f} (improved={improved}, "
              f"t={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s)")

        if no_cycle_improve >= 3:
            print("  No improvement in several cycles, stopping cycle phase.")
            break

    print(f"After phase 2: best = {best_sc:.8f} (t={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s)")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 3: final polishing
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    final_end = hard_deadline - 8.0

    if time.monotonic() < final_end:
        t_end = min(final_end, time.monotonic() + 120.0)
        a0, sc0 = active_polish_self(normalize1(best), t_end,
                                     max_iters=5000, stall_limit=150, verbose=True)
        if np.isfinite(sc0) and sc0 < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best = a0.copy()
            best_sc = sc0

    if time.monotonic() < final_end - 60.0:
        t_end = min(final_end - 30.0, time.monotonic() + 180.0)
        a1, sc1 = run_flux_lbfgs(normalize1(best), t_end,
                                 betas=(100.0, 300.0, 1000.0, 3000.0), verbose=True)
        if np.isfinite(sc1) and sc1 < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best = a1.copy()
            best_sc = sc1

    if time.monotonic() < final_end - 45.0:
        t_end = min(final_end - 20.0, time.monotonic() + 90.0)
        a2, sc2 = adam_flux_polish(normalize1(best), 500.0, t_end,
                                   max_iters=600, lr=0.05, verbose=True)
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best = a2.copy()
            best_sc = sc2

    if time.monotonic() < final_end - 20.0:
        t_end = min(final_end, time.monotonic() + 40.0)
        a3, sc3 = active_polish_self(normalize1(best), t_end,
                                     max_iters=300, stall_limit=60, verbose=False)
        if np.isfinite(sc3) and sc3 < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best = a3.copy()
            best_sc = sc3

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Output construction
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, float(v)))) for v in np.asarray(best, dtype=np.float64).ravel()]
    if len(out) == 0 or sum(out) < 0.01:
        out = [0.5] * 64

    verified = np.nan
    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
    except Exception as exc:
        verified = np.nan
        print("Final evaluate_sequence failed:", exc)

    if not np.isfinite(verified):
        out = [0.5] * 64

    print("=" * 60)
    print(f"FINAL internal score = {best_sc:.8f}")
    print(f"FINAL verified score = {verified if np.isfinite(verified) else 'nan'}")
    print(f"Length = {len(out)}, elapsed = {time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s")
    print("=" * 60)

    return out
```
